THE APPOINTMENT PUBLISHED AGAINST THE SUSPICION [claim EX36-01]. SEE, the LORD has EXALTED BY NAME Bezalel — the naming re-announced to the PEOPLE, the endowment upgraded to a spirit of PROPHECY from before the LORD (Onkelos Exod 35). The spine records the audit-charge: Moses is king, Aaron high priest, his sons deputies, Eleazar chief, the Kohathites bearers — and now the works contract to the kinsman! — and the answer: 'I did nothing of my own,' AND HE SHOWS THEM THE VERSE (Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 3:2); Moses himself learned the builder's name only at handoff, having thought every THOU-SHALT-MAKE meant him (Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 3:1). Seated under the amended spine, auto-seat law. 		—Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 3:1, Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 3:2, Onkelos Exod 35witnessed

THE BUILDING RUNS ON THE WORLD'S OWN TOOLCHAIN [claim EX36-04]. Every wise-hearted man in whom the LORD put wisdom and understanding takes the materials, each man from his task (Onkelos Exod 36) — and the spine states the identity: wisdom, understanding, knowledge are the THREE ATTRIBUTES BY WHICH THE WORLD WAS CREATED (the LORD by wisdom founded the earth), and with the same three the tabernacle was built (Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 5:1) — as the Temple after it and the house to come: one toolchain, four builds (Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 5:2). Wisdom granted to ALL the workers — the ink pressed to include even the beasts (Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 4:7); the merit lines ride: Hur's martyrdom paid in the grandson's commission (Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 4:6), Miriam's midwife-wisdom the mother-line (Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 4:10). Seated under the amended spine, auto-seat law. 		—Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 4:10, Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 4:6, Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 4:7, Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 5:1, Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 5:2, Onkelos Exod 36witnessed

THE OVERFLOW PROTOCOL [claim EX36-05]. The craftsmen report upward — the people bring TOO MUCH; Moses orders a herald through the camp: let no man or woman do any more work for the sacred offering — AND THE PEOPLE STOPPED; and the audit line closes the intake: ENOUGH for all the work, AND SOME LEFT OVER (Onkelos Exod 36). R. Yohanan counts the whole intake at TWO MORNINGS from the doubled morning-word (Midrash Tanchuma, Pekudei 5:4). The case shelf hangs the CARRYING labor from this very stop order — the camp-wide ban on bringing from the tents is the Sabbath carrying-out's derivation anchor (Mishnah Shevuot 1:1, this exam's docket). 		—Midrash Tanchuma, Pekudei 5:4, Mishnah Shevuot 1:1, Onkelos Exod 36witnessed
WITNESS_READWITNESS-READ(camp_proclamation, carrying_out_ink)
= WITNESS-READ(camp-proclamation, carrying-out-ink)
THE PROCLAMATION'S TWO EXPORTS [claim EX36-08]. The round-17 block opens the derivation EX36-05 anticipated: R. Yochanan — WHERE IS CARRYING-OUT ITSELF WRITTEN? In this verse's ויעבירו קול במחנה ("and they passed a proclamation through the camp"): Moses sat in the Levites' camp, the public domain, and told Israel — do not carry out from the private domain to it. The SHABBAT-DAY proof: against the completed-intake alternative (36:7), העברה-העברה ("passing-passing") from the Jubilee shofar (Lev 25:9) — a banned-labor day both. And the SAME analogy runs the other way: the Jubilee shofar's "passing" learns from Moses' proclamation to be WITH A VOICE — this verse the fixed end of both directions. The height rider: the sons of Kehat carried ABOVE TEN handbreadths (the ten-cubit boards; Moses spreading the tent; the pole-load's third), so height does not exempt the carrier; the typical-manner gate (right hand, left, lap, shoulder — as Kehat) rides the same Tabernacle precedent. Auto-seat law, F-064. 		—Shabbat 96b:1, Shabbat 96b:2, Shabbat 96b:3, Rosh Hashanah 34a:17, Shabbat 92a:5, Shabbat 92a:6, Shabbat 92a:7witnessed

= WITNESS-READ(standing-boards, pledge-and-fruit-tree)
THE BOARDS: STANDING, UNFRUITFUL, AND IN PAWN [claim EX36-06]. The planks STANDING upright (Onkelos Exod 36) — the held-as-they-grow token executed; the spine adds two laws on the species and the name: the King to whom all belongs built from NON-fruit-bearing acacia — how much more must a man spare fruit trees (Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 9:1); and the building's name read as PLEDGE — if Israel incurs guilt the tabernacle is seized as their collateral, and when no house stands, the righteous are taken as the pledge (Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 9:2). Seated under the amended spine, auto-seat law. 		—Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 9:1, Midrash Tanchuma, Vayakhel 9:2, Onkelos Exod 36witnessed

= WITNESS-READ(veil-made, case-shelf-spec-recited)
THE VEIL, WITH THE CASE SHELF'S OWN NUMBERS [claim EX36-07]. The dividing veil made — craftsman's work with cherubim, the embroiderer's screen at the door: two textile grades, inside and threshold (Onkelos Exod 36). And the Buber recension recites the case shelf's spec-sheet INTO the reading: a handbreadth thick, woven on SEVENTY-TWO warps at TWENTY-FOUR threads each, forty cubits by twenty, no knots, two made yearly, THREE HUNDRED priests to immerse it (Midrash Tanchuma Buber, Vayakhel 10:1 — the Temple-veil table of Mishnah Shekalim 8:4 and its neighbor), with the Buber-only order note that MOSES made the veil before Bezalel took the ark. Numeric claims opened in full. Seated under the amended spine, auto-seat law. 		—Midrash Tanchuma Buber, Vayakhel 10:1, Mishnah Shekalim 8:4, Onkelos Exod 36witnessed
